IEEE TGRS · Crossref 发现源新登记 147 条

Crossref（ISSN 0196-2892 / eISSN 1558-0644，按 created 补全至上次轮检之后）另有 147 条未在本次官方 Early Access 前两页题名中匹配到的新 DOI，部分已带 Volume 64 元数据。仅作发现/元数据登记，不以发现源关闭年度卷。官方 Current Volume 已核验仍为开放中的 2,608 篇。

International Journal of Intelligent Systems · ONLINE_FIRST

MobFuzz: Adaptive Multiobjective Optimization in Gray-Box Fuzzing

Gen Zhang; Pengfei Wang; Shan Huang; Xinhai Chen; Lingbin Zeng

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1155/int/3696726
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

MobFuzz: Adaptive Multiobjective Optimization in Gray-Box Fuzzing

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/3696726 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Coverage‐guided gray‐box fuzzing (CGF) must pursue useful program behaviors while preserving the execution capacity needed to test many inputs. This article examines MobFuzz, a multiobjective CGF design, and its port to the American Fuzzy Lop++ framework (AFL++), called MobFuzz++. MobFuzz represents objective combinations and seed‐allocation decisions with a multiplayer multiarmed bandit (MPMAB) model. It then uses a nondominated sorting genetic algorithm in CGF (NIC) to improve the selected objectives while sharing useful candidates with the main fuzzing loop. We evaluate the inherited design on 12 real‐world programs and the MAGMA dataset and evaluate the AFL++ implementation on UniBench. Across these evaluations, the reported objective improvements reach 107%, and energy consumption is reduced by up to 55% in the corresponding comparisons. MobFuzz achieves up to 2x more program coverage and finds 3x more unique bugs than the baseline fuzzers. NIC improves the good‐seed measure by at least 2x with a measured execution‐speed cost of approximately 3%. The UniBench experiments additionally compare AFL, AFL++, MobFuzz, and MobFuzz++ and include an AFL++ ablation of MPMAB and NIC.

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 论文页面

返回页首

International Journal of Intelligent Systems · ONLINE_FIRST

Research on Intelligent Manufacturing Production Line Scheduling Optimization Based on Improved Gray Wolf Optimization Algorithm

Yijia Xu; Jindong Zhao; Yicheng Xu; Shuocheng Sun; Zhe Yang

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1155/int/3955742
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Research on Intelligent Manufacturing Production Line Scheduling Optimization Based on Improved Gray Wolf Optimization Algorithm

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/3955742 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Intelligent manufacturing production line scheduling is formulated as a flexible job shop problem that minimizes the makespan and the total energy consumption. The classical gray wolf optimizer handles this problem poorly because its continuous update does not match the discrete schedule, and it also suffers from random initialization, a fixed local search and late stagnation. This study proposes QM‐IGWO, a Q‐learning–driven multistrategy collaborative improved gray wolf optimizer. Random keys map every wolf to a feasible operation sequence and machine assignment, and an NEH insertion adapted to the flexible job shop seeds both strings. A Q‐learning agent observes the Hamming diversity of the decoded strings and the stagnation level, selects one of four neighborhoods for a local search on the three leaders, and shares the stagnation signal with a selective restart that keeps the Q‐table and an external archive of nondominated schedules. On the Brandimarte benchmark, QM‐IGWO shortens the mean makespan of the classical optimizer by 11.1% and matches or improves the best makespans of a recent hybrid gray wolf optimizer on all ten instances with less than one‐sixth of its evaluation budget. It also leads on the three Hurink subsets, raises the hypervolume by 14.2% over the classical optimizer and by 5.3% over a recent Q‐learning gray wolf optimizer, and shortens the makespan of two automotive parts production lines by 12.3% and 13.1% with about 10% less energy. Holm‐corrected Wilcoxon tests find it significantly better on 94% of the instance‐baseline pairs and never worse.

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 论文页面

An Argumentation-Based System for Classifying Driving Behaviors

B. Fazzinga; F. Furfaro; G. Monterosso

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控
NEW_ARTICLE

论文题名

An Argumentation-Based System for Classifying Driving Behaviors

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/7024855 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

We propose a framework for the automated classification of driving behavior, leveraging time‐series data collected by inertial sensors during vehicular trips. The framework processes the sensor readings in order to generate a driving style certificate, which provides a characterization of the behavior exhibited by the driver throughout the entire trip, assigning it to one of three predefined categories: Calm, Normal, or Aggressive. The classification process is governed by a reasoner based on the Abstract Argumentation Framework, a formalism of the field of artificial intelligence designed to model and resolve conflicting information among competing agents. In this context, the selection of the appropriate driving style at each time point is modeled as the outcome of an argumentative process, where agents, each associated with a specific classification hypothesis, engage in structured interactions that may support or challenge the claims of others, based on both interagent dynamics and evidence derived from sensor readings. A prototype implementing the proposed methodology has been developed, and its performance has been assessed through experimental validation using real‐world driving data.

All-in-One Video Restoration With Adaptive Degradation Perception and Global–Local Synergy

Deqian Mao; Shanshan Gao; Xinchen Jiao; Xunxiang Yao; Yuanfeng Zhou

OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

All-in-One Video Restoration With Adaptive Degradation Perception and Global–Local Synergy

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/8330880 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

All‐in‐one video restoration aims to restore degraded videos with different degradation types using a unified algorithm or model. However, existing video restoration methods have limited perception of video degradation types, making them unable to flexibly adapt to diverse degradation scenarios. Meanwhile, inaccurate calculation of cross‐temporal features between video frames affects the accuracy of video restoration. To address these issues, we propose an all‐in‐one video restoration method (DPGA‐VR) via adaptive degradation perception and global–local synergy, which fully mines the global–local representations between video frames and adaptively perceives different degradation types in degraded videos, breaking the traditional paradigm of “single model processing single degradation.” Specifically, a spatiotemporal feature encoder based on a local transformer accurately mines and extracts the global–local spatiotemporal features of the degraded video, achieving cross‐temporal perception between video frames. The adaptive degradation perceptron uses different “experts” to flexibly handle videos with different degradation types. In addition, the global–local synergy reconstruction can maximize the global–local consistency of the restored video. Extensive experiments on video deraining and dehazing tasks demonstrate that DPGA‐VR exhibits good all‐in‐one video restoration performance.

Dynamic Adversarial Feature Fusion for Tennis Win-Rate Prediction: A Comparative Machine-Learning Study

Sheng Wang; Rongke Lv; Ming Liu; Qiang; Shangqi Nie; Hyongjun Choi

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Dynamic Adversarial Feature Fusion for Tennis Win-Rate Prediction: A Comparative Machine-Learning Study

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/4030743 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Traditional tennis win‐rate models rely on static technical statistics and, therefore, fail to capture the dynamic, adversarial nature of professional matches. To address this gap, we propose DAF‐ADA, a dynamic adversarial feature‐fusion framework that couples multisource sensor data (Hawk‐Eye trajectories, inertial‐sensor metrics, and plantar pressure) with a biomechanically constrained generative adversarial network for tactical‐mutation augmentation, an LSTM–attention temporal extractor, and dynamic weighting with psychological‐resilience quantification. On 320 clay‐court matches by 20 players (64,000 analysis windows) with a held‐out player split, DAF‐ADA achieved 57.1 ± 1.8% accuracy, 2.4 percentage points above the industry benchmark and on par with the strongest baseline (LSTM). Under five adversarial perturbation regimes, its mean accuracy (54.0%) was comparable to that of LSTM (54.5%), with a relative advantage under extreme serve‐speed perturbations (50.7% vs. 49.4%). These results provide a fully documented, reproducible benchmark for win‐rate prediction under player‐level generalization and show that biomechanically constrained adversarial augmentation and dynamic feature fusion yield competitive accuracy and near‐real‐time inference, while uniform robustness gains over strong sequence baselines are not obtained on this dataset.

Supervised Neighborhood Rough Set With Adaptive Neighborhood Granularity for Multilabel Feature Selection

Shuwen Zheng; Guoping Lin; Yujie Qin

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Supervised Neighborhood Rough Set With Adaptive Neighborhood Granularity for Multilabel Feature Selection

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/6276442 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Multilabel learning involves scenarios where each instance is associated with multiple labels simultaneously. Existing supervised neighborhood rough set methods typically rely on binary supervision, which fail to capture partial label consistency between samples in multilabel data. Moreover, the neighborhood radius is often manually specified, making neighborhood construction less adaptive to different data distributions. To address these issues, we propose a continuously supervised neighborhood approximation scheme for multilabel feature selection. Specifically, a label consistency function is introduced to quantify the semantic similarity between instances according to their associated labels. Based on this function, an adaptive neighborhood radius estimation strategy is developed to determine the radius automatically from the data. Subsequently, the supervised neighborhood rough set framework is further generalized to the multilabel setting, followed by an analysis of its fundamental properties. Building on these components, an integrated feature selection scheme is established. Experimental results on several benchmark multilabel datasets show that the proposed method can produce smaller yet effective feature subsets, while achieving predictive performance comparable to or better than that of existing competitive approaches, thereby demonstrating its effectiveness for multilabel feature selection.

Graph Neural Network and Deep Reinforcement Learning Driven Kill Chain Construction: Supporting Cross-Domain Task Allocation Decision System

Xiangyu Liu; Gang Wang; Siyuan Wang

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1155/int/4673384
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Graph Neural Network and Deep Reinforcement Learning Driven Kill Chain Construction: Supporting Cross-Domain Task Allocation Decision System

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/4673384 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

At present, the decoupling and distributed deployment of combat resources result in low real‐time performance and poor reliability of autonomous task allocation decision‐making systems during resource scheduling. Traditional centralized task allocation methods are unable to address these challenges with adequate flexibility and adaptability. To solve the above problems, this paper proposes a kill chain construction method combined with graph neural networks (GNNs) and deep reinforcement learning so as to support the efficient operation of trans‐domain task allocation decision‐making systems. First, to effectively capture the information interactions, interdependent coupling relationships, combat capabilities, and core objectives of heterogeneous combat resources in air–ground cooperative kill chain construction, an RM kill chain model is designed. The theoretical advantages of the reverse‐sequence logic and multilayer architecture are theoretically analyzed under explicit assumptions and experimentally validated from both the construction logic and hierarchical architecture perspectives. Subsequently, tailored to the inherent characteristics of the proposed RM model, the dual‐stage GACP‐KM algorithm is developed. In the first stage, node feature learning and adaptive partitioning are achieved based on GNNs and multi‐Actor‐Critic architecture. In the second stage, the Kuhn–Munkres (KM) algorithm is employed within each partition to achieve optimal construction of kill chains. In particular, a factorized partitioning action space, a partition‐number‐invariant scoring network, and a two‐level reward driven by the final construction quality are designed to tightly couple the learning‐based partitioning stage with the exact matching stage. Finally, the proposed methods are applied and validated in cross‐domain cooperative combat scenarios. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate the superiority of the RM model, the computational efficiency and solution quality of the GACP‐KM algorithm, and the strong compatibility between the model and algorithm.

Schrödinger-Based Path Planning: A Wave-Diffraction Approach to Global Navigation in Complex Environments

Santiago Garrido; Fernando Quevedo; Ramon Barber; Luis Moreno

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1155/int/8381325
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Schrödinger-Based Path Planning: A Wave-Diffraction Approach to Global Navigation in Complex Environments

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/8381325 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This paper presents a novel approach to global path planning and autonomous navigation utilizing the steady‐state Schrödinger equation. Traditional heuristic search algorithms, such as A ∗ and RRT ∗ , often become computationally prohibitive in dynamic environments or when smooth trajectories are required. In contrast, the proposed framework treats the workspace as a continuous physical system governed by a partial differential equation (PDE). By modeling the target configuration as a source term and obstacles as high‐potential barriers within a Hamiltonian operator, the algorithm yields a global wave function Ψ that simultaneously encodes the reachability of every point in the workspace. To overcome numerical instabilities associated with exponential decay and vanishing gradients, we apply the Cole–Hopf transformation, mapping the wave intensity onto a robust potential manifold U . Furthermore, a normalized gradient descent scheme is introduced to maintain a constant traversal velocity and prevent agent stagnation in regions of low wave amplitude. Experimental evaluations in 2D and 3D environments demonstrate that the resulting navigation field generates smooth, C ∞ ‐continuous, collision‐free trajectories for multiagent systems in near real time. Ultimately, this physics‐informed formulation provides a continuous, smooth, and scalable alternative to discrete graph‐search techniques, offering inherent obstacle avoidance and natural scalability for multirobot navigation.

Explainable Multitype Anomaly Detection Using a Lightweight Fully Convolutional Architecture

Alex George; Lyudmila Mihaylova; Sean R. Anderson

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控

10.1155/int/4983395

OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Explainable Multitype Anomaly Detection Using a Lightweight Fully Convolutional Architecture

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/4983395 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Explainable anomaly detection methods can identify and localize anomalies within images but typically cannot distinguish anomaly types. Furthermore, they often require separate models for each object category, increasing training and maintenance costs. Recent vision‐language models address this problem but remain computationally expensive. To address this, we propose Multitype Fully Convolutional Data Description (MultiTypeFCDD), a unified, lightweight convolutional approach to explainable anomaly detection that trains on image‐level labels yet predicts type‐specific spatial heatmaps across multiple objects from a single model. Our primary contribution is a novel multichannel model, discriminative loss and training framework, enabling decoupled representations of co‐occurring defects while predicting anomaly maps in a single forward pass. We evaluated the method on the Real Industrial Anomaly Detection (Real‐IAD) manufacturing dataset, achieving 96.4% image‐level Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) curve, and the Water Research Centre (WRc) sewer defect dataset, achieving 92.2% image‐level AUROC. The model uses just over 1% of the parameter count of state‐of‐the‐art vision‐language models used for similar tasks and achieves a tenfold speed‐up over Gradient‐Weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad‐CAM) (9.4 ms versus 94.7 ms) by avoiding computationally expensive backward passes. These results demonstrate the efficiency and adaptability of our framework across diverse industrial domains.

Personalized Automated Test Generation in Programming Education Using Fuzzy-Weighted Large Language Models

Christos Troussas; Christos Papakostas; Akrivi Krouska; Phivos Mylonas; Cleo Sgouropoulou

卷期
V2026 · I1
门控
NEW_ARTICLE

10.1155/int/9444834
证据层级
OFFICIAL_DIRECTORY

官方论文页面onlinelibrary.wiley.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Personalized Automated Test Generation in Programming Education Using Fuzzy-Weighted Large Language Models

期刊：International Journal of Intelligent Systems · DOI：10.1155/int/9444834 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

The rapid adoption of large language models (LLMs) has stimulated research into automated assessment generation, yet most existing approaches either rely on generic prompts that overlook learner diversity or apply traditional personalization methods detached from generative AI. This creates a compelling need for frameworks that combine the linguistic flexibility of LLMs with principled learner modeling to deliver assessments that are both adaptive and psychometrically reliable. This paper introduces a generative‐adaptive assessment paradigm that integrates fuzzy reasoning with LLMs to address the content‐exhaustion limitations of traditional item‐bank‐based adaptive testing systems. Learner proficiency is first estimated through a curriculum‐aligned diagnostic assessment and normalized to the interval [0, 1]. Trapezoidal membership functions, elicited from domain experts, transform these scores into fuzzy weights representing low, medium, and high proficiency. These weights are then injected directly into the ChatGPT prompt via the OpenAI application programming interface (API), shaping the target distribution of item difficulty during generation. The system architecture connects a learner interface, a fuzzy‐weighted personalization layer, the LLM, and an item bank with automated validation filters, ensuring that generated assessments are coherent, balanced, and auditable. The contribution of this work lies in the operational integration of fuzzy learner modeling with LLM‐based item generation through an explicit and auditable mapping from proficiency estimates to target difficulty distributions. The framework brings these components together within an end‐to‐end assessment pipeline that combines generation, personalization, validation, and empirical evaluation. Analysis of data from a controlled classroom evaluation involving 282 undergraduate students across multiple course sections demonstrated that the proposed approach significantly improved learning performance, assessment reliability, and perceived fairness compared to both manual assessment design and nonpersonalized LLM‐based generation.

Diego Cortes; Jeff Dominitz; Maximiliano Romero

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Partial identification of mean achievement in ILSA studies with multi-stage stratified sample designs and student non-participation

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf209 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

International large-scale assessment (ILSA) studies collect information across education systems with the objective of learning about the population-wide distribution of student achievement in the assessment. In this article, we study one of the most fundamental threats that these studies face when justifying the conclusions reached about these distributions: the identification problem that arises from student non-participation during data collection. Recognizing that ILSA studies have traditionally employed a narrow range of strategies to address non-participation, we examine this problem using tools developed within the framework of partial identification of probability distributions. We tailor this framework to the problem of non-participation when data are collected using a multi-stage stratified random sample design, as in most ILSA studies. We demonstrate this approach with application to the International Computer and Information Literacy Study in 2018. We show how to use the framework to assess mean achievement under reasonable and credible sets of assumptions about the non-participating population. We also provide examples of how these results may be reported by agencies that administer ILSA studies. By doing so, we bring to the field of ILSA an alternative strategy for identification, estimation, and reporting of population parameters of interest.

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

A network approach to detect Value Added Tax fraud

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf205 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Value Added Tax (VAT) fraud erodes public revenue and puts legitimate businesses at a disadvantaged position thereby exacerbating inequality. This article develops scalable algorithms to detect fraudulent transactions by leveraging the rich information embedded in the complex, high-dimensional VAT network structure. Supervised methods are not always suitable for VAT fraud detection, as issues in the auditing process—such as selection bias and audit quality—can seriously affect the labelling of businesses as fraudsters or not. Therefore, both supervised and unsupervised techniques in which VAT fraud detection is implemented through a suitably constructed Laplacian matrix informed by business-specific covariates. The developed methods are applied to the universe of Bulgarian VAT data and detect around 50% of the VAT fraud, outperforming well-known techniques that ignore the information provided by the transactional network structure. The proposed methods are automated and can be implemented following taxpayers’ submission of their VAT returns, thus allowing the authorities to prevent large revenue losses.

论文题名

An Age–Period–Cohort model for the gender gap in youth and early adult mortality

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf207 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

In this article, we introduce a novel framework in mortality risk study, operating on the statistical approach of the Age–Period–Cohort (APC) framework by leveraging the skew-normal distribution and Bayesian estimation. We propose a specific application to gender gap analysis and forecasting. By employing data from the Human Mortality Database, our study contributes first, a novel perspective on gender gap analysis and forecasting and, second, an improvement to the statistical framework for APC analysis. To test our approach, we apply the proposed model in three different mortality scenarios and compare our projection with a Bayesian APC model as a benchmark. The results show that the proposed framework has the potential to gain efficiency and accuracy in forecast gender differences in mortality. By offering a systematic approach to quantifying and forecasting the gender gap across different age groups and time periods, our results may help researchers and policymakers to better understand underlying trends and temporal dynamics.

Modelling the spatially varying nonlinear effects of heat exposure

Xinyi Chen; Marta Blangiardo; Connor Gascoigne; Garyfallos Konstantinoudis

RSS_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Modelling the spatially varying nonlinear effects of heat exposure

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf208 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Exposure to high ambient temperatures is a significant driver of preventable mortality, with nonlinear health effects and elevated risks in specific regions. To capture this complexity and account for spatial dependencies across small areas, we propose a Bayesian framework that integrates nonlinear functions with the Besag, York, and Mollie model. Applying this framework to all-cause mortality data in Switzerland, we quantified spatial inequalities in heat-related mortality. We retrieved daily all-cause mortality at small areas (2,145 municipalities) for people older than 65 years from the Swiss Federal Office of Public Health and daily mean temperature at 1km×1km grid from the Swiss Federal Office of Meteorology. By fully propagating uncertainties, we derived key epidemiological metrics, including heat-related excess mortality and minimum mortality temperature (MMT). Heat-related excess mortality rates were higher in northern Switzerland, while lower MMTs were observed in mountainous regions. Further, we explored the role of the proportion of individuals older than 85 years, green space, average temperature, deprivation, urbanicity, air pollution, and language regions in explaining these discrepancies. We found that spatial disparities in heat-related excess mortality were primarily driven by population age distribution, green space, and vulnerabilities associated with elevated temperature exposure.

Multivariate mixed models accounting for don’t know options in ordinal data

Ralitza Gueorguieva; Maria Iannario

卷期
V189 · I4
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf197
证据层级
RSS_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Multivariate mixed models accounting for don’t know options in ordinal data

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf197 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Multivariate ordinal data are common in surveys measuring perceptions or knowledge. Frequently, a ‘don’t know’ option is also available resulting in partially ordinal scales. This complicates modelling: standard ordinal models become inappropriate whereas nominal models are inefficient and difficult to interpret. Simply discarding the ‘don’t know’ responses may lead to bias as responders who choose to provide ratings often differ systematically from those who do not. This manuscript proposes a unified solution that jointly models the selection of ‘don’t know’ options and the ordinal ratings on multiple variables. The proposed multivariate mixed models are flexible, allow for heterogeneity, response styles, and covariate effects both on the ordinal ratings and on choosing the ‘don’t know’ options. Likelihood-based inference and model comparisons are performed. Two case studies: one on financial risk perceptions and one on knowledge about the addictiveness of tobacco products are used for motivation and illustration. A simulation demonstrates that the proposed approach yields unbiased and efficient estimates. The methodology provides interpretable results while capturing the complexity of partially ordinal data. It offers a robust framework for analysing survey responses in social and behavioural research, where ‘don’t know’ options and heterogeneous rating behavior are pervasive.

Inference and diagnosis in M-quantile models with applications to small area estimation

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Inference and diagnosis in M-quantile models with applications to small area estimation

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf206 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This paper advances the use of M-quantile (MQ) regression models in small area estimation, addressing issues related to inference and diagnosis in MQ models. First, we prove the consistency of the area-specific MQ coefficients, which are the equivalent of random effects in mixed models to capture the variability between areas. We then turn to the analysis of the residuals, approximating their distribution and drawing parallels with linear models. Finally, we estimate the distribution of the optimal robustness parameters for bias correction by bootstrap and present some empirical results. In this way, it is possible to formulate a test for the detection of atypical areas. Simulation experiments validate the methodology’s effectiveness for outlier detection and diagnostics. An application to Spanish income data illustrates the practical relevance of these methods, highlighting their value for targeted policy analysis and diagnostics.

Multiresolution neural networks for electricity market forecasting: addressing complexity with q-wavelet methods

Foued Saâdaoui; Hana Rabbouch

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Multiresolution neural networks for electricity market forecasting: addressing complexity with q-wavelet methods

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf202 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This article introduces a multiresolution neural network framework for forecasting electricity spot prices, exemplified by the Australian market’s mature and intricate structure. Central to our methodology is the q-Maximal Overlap Discrete Wavelet Transform, an advanced preprocessing technique integrated into a feedforward neural network (FFNN) to capture long-memory components and enhance feature representation. Unlike traditional wavelet-based methods, our approach consolidates processed sub-signals into regular and irregular components, modelled separately using specialized FFNNs to improve computational efficiency. The inclusion of dummy variables, such as Daylight Saving Time, further supports the integration of exogenous factors, enhancing robustness and interpretability. The proposal achieves improvements of up to 20-fold over a conventional single-hidden-layer FFNN, two- to 15-fold over univariate state-of-the-art models, and two- to more than ninefold over comparable bivariate models, underscoring its accuracy and stability. These results establish the framework as a decision-support tool for navigating the uncertainty and heterogeneity of modern energy systems while advancing operational research methodologies and promoting efficient, data-driven strategies for energy transitions.

Putting female jockeys in their place

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf200 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Gender bias and discrimination within labour markets is a significant area of concern in many industries yet it represents an inherently difficult field for empirical analysis. Infrequent hiring events and complexities in obtaining truly objective measures of productivity, often coupled with under-representation of females, severely limits data availability in most workplace settings. Using a novel approach, we take advantage of the frequent hiring events within the sport of horse racing to evaluate productivity against opportunities afforded to individual jockeys. Our ranking model identifies under- and over-valued jockeys, and we find that women are significantly more likely to be under-used than males, in an industry in which performance data are readily available.

Théo Voldoire; Robin J Ryder; Ryan Lahfa

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Some are observed, all leave traces: whole-population modelling of French elite civil servants’ career paths

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf199 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Elite civil servants may move between the public and private sectors throughout their career, a process of interest for the public and social scientists. However, data on career paths are rarely completely available, calling for inference tools that can handle many missing values. We consider public–private paths of elite French civil servants and introduce binary Markov switching models with Bayesian data augmentation. Our procedure combines two complementary data sources: (1) detailed observations of some individual trajectories collected from LinkedIn and (2) less informative ‘traces’ left by all individuals in administrative records, which we model for missing data imputation. This framework allows for varying parameters across individuals and time, yet maintains properties of hidden Markov models, enabling posterior exploration with a tailored sampler. By integrating both sources, we can consider the whole population rather than just a sample, and avoid biases that arise when using only a single source. This allows us to properly test substantive hypotheses on career paths across different public organizations. We notably show that the probability ENA graduates exit the public sector has not increased since 1990, but the probability of return has increased. We identify four clusters of organizations, with distinct patterns of public–private behaviours.

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Investigating impacts of health policies using staggered difference-in-differences: the effects of adoption of an online consultation system on prescribing patterns of antibiotics

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf198 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Since the COVID-19 pandemic, most general practices in England have adopted online consultation (OC) systems for easier access and demand management. However, there are concerns that easier access could increase demand relating to low-risk minor illness, with consequential changes in prescribing patterns. Using GP practice-level data we investigate effects of adoption of an OC system in English general practice on antibiotic prescribing rates. The target estimand is the average effect of adoption on the outcome in each month post-adoption within groups of practices defined by month of adoption. Estimation is using a doubly robust staggered difference-in-differences approach. We also estimate an effect aggregated across all adopting practices and across time since adoption. Overall, we find evidence that adoption of an OC system results in higher antibiotic prescribing rates, with our primary analysis indicating an overall 5.1% increase. However, using recently proposed methods, we find that our estimates are sensitive to possible violations of the key identification assumption, conditional parallel trends. When allowing for relatively small violations of this assumption, we would no longer conclude that there is statistically significant evidence of an effect and smaller in magnitude effects become more plausible, which may be considered reassuring for health policy.

Statistical early warning models with applications

Lucas Harlaar; Jacques Commandeur; Jan van den Brakel; Siem Jan Koopman; Niels Bos; Frits Bijleveld

RSS_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Statistical early warning models with applications

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf193 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This paper investigates the feasibility of using earlier provisional data to improve the now- and forecasting accuracy of final and official statistics. We propose the use of a multivariate structural time series model, which includes common trends and seasonal components to combine official statistics series with related auxiliary series. In this way, more precise and more timely nowcasts and forecasts of official statistics can be obtained by exploiting the higher frequency and/or the more timely availability of the auxiliary series. The proposed method can be applied to different data sources consisting of any number of missing observations both at the beginning and at the end of the series simultaneously. Two empirical applications are presented. The first one focuses on fatal traffic accidents and the second one on labour force participation at the municipal level. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed approach in improving forecasting performance for the target series and providing early warnings to policy-makers.

论文题名

Asylum-seekers at the extremes

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf196 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This article conducts a first-hand study on forecasting asylum-seekers’ applications across seven major host countries, i.e. the USA, Germany, France, Italy, Spain, Austria, and Greece by 2027. Poisson regression is used for flow forecasting and Extreme Value Theory for the maximum monthly number of applications. As for the latter, we first employ a static version of the Peaks-over-Threshold approach, before modelling the possible non-stationarity of the exceedances via a Generalized Pareto Distribution with time-varying parameters. Overall, the findings are in line with one another and can provide a useful road map to policymakers.

Randomization inference for before-and-after studies with multiple units: an application to a criminal procedure reform in Uruguay

Matias D Cattaneo; Carlos Díaz; Rocío Titiunik

卷期
V189 · I4
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf192
证据层级
RSS_DISCOVERY

官方论文页面academic.oup.com · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Randomization inference for before-and-after studies with multiple units: an application to a criminal procedure reform in Uruguay

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnaf192 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Learning about the immediate causal effects of large-scale policy interventions poses a significant challenge for quasi-experimental methods that rely on long-term trends or parametric modelling assumptions. As an alternative, we develop a randomization inference framework for before-and-after studies with multiple units, designed specifically for short-term causal inference and allowing for general assignment mechanisms. The method provides finite-sample-valid statistical inferences without relying on parametric time series models or extrapolation. We demonstrate its utility by analyzing a major criminal justice reform in Uruguay that switched from an inquisitorial to an adversarial system in November 2017. Our method relies on the key assumption of no local time trends near the policy adoption time, which is supported by several falsification tests in our empirical study. We find a statistically significant short-term causal effect: an increase of approximately 25 daily police reports (an 8% rise) in the first week of the new justice system. Our randomization inference framework provides a robust and flexible methodology for evaluating policy adoptions in before-and-after studies with multiple units.

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Nonparametric bounds on informal workers’ share

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag125 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This paper develops a nonparametric measurement framework for bounding the prevalence of informal (dependent) employment by leveraging aggregate, publicly available and timely statistics that are maintained in most countries. The method employs partial identification techniques to derive bounds on the proportion of informal workers by comparing aggregate data from official statistics with public-use Social Security records. Applied to Italian data, under minimal assumptions, this approach yields credible estimates with narrow bounds: informal employment remained stable at 10–12% until 2020 but declined to 8–10% in the post-pandemic period. As official estimates indicate, informal employment is more prevalent in southern regions, though it has followed a downward trend. Notably, before a recent methodological revision, official estimates may have overstated the true rate in several instances.

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Are new parties shut out of the UK House of Commons?

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag124 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

In recent years, it has been claimed that new political parties are shut out of the UK House of Commons because they are rarely elected. At the same time, general political understanding seems to be that the House of Commons is currently further away from a pure two-party distribution of seats than at any time in the past century, perhaps longer. Although this might seem like a paradox it is possible for both of these things to have credence. We present an analysis that shows that the number of new political parties elected to the House of Commons has been slowly declining over the last century. We illustrate this both by regression and Good-Turing analyses. We also analyse the lifetime and persistence of political parties as well as present metrics that indicate the recent increased political diversity and identify the nadir of diversity in the mid-1960s and early 1970s.

A hidden Markov model for disease trend analysis with joinpoints

Hyoyoung Choo-Wosoba; Paul S Albert

卷期
V · I—
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnag119
证据层级
CROSSREF_METADATA

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

A hidden Markov model for disease trend analysis with joinpoints

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag119 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

The joinpoint model assumes piece-wise linear curves joined at multiple unknown joinpoints where the slope changes and has been widely used for disease surveillance. There are 2 analytical challenges that cannot be addressed with standard joinpoint methods. First, computation becomes infeasible as the number of potential joinpoints become large. Second, assessing the uncertainty that a joinpoint occurs at a particular time or window of time. We propose a hidden Markov model (HMM) that provides a computationally feasible approach to deal with these challenges. We show that it is natural to formulate the joinpoint model by modelling successive differences in observations as an HMM with the Markov chain being specified as a birth-process where each state change reflects the occurrence of a subsequent joinpoint. An extension of the forward–backward algorithm, developed for maximum-likelihood estimation of HMMs, easily accommodates long disease sequences with a large number of joinpoints. Further, we show how functions of the posterior distribution of state occupancy can be used to access joinpoint uncertainty. We show simulations with various settings and an application study of COVID-19 incidence data.

How reliable are self-reports of debt? Misclassification, selection, and measurement bias between survey and administrative data in the UK Household Longitudinal Study

Eliud Kibuchi; Daniel Kopasker; Jonathan Stokes; Alastair H Leyland; Srinivasa Vittal Katikireddi

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

How reliable are self-reports of debt? Misclassification, selection, and measurement bias between survey and administrative data in the UK Household Longitudinal Study

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag123 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Self-reported and administratively recorded debt measures are susceptible to measurement error, while selection bias may arise if individuals who consent to data linkage differ systematically from those who do not. This study evaluates the quality of self-reported and administrative debt measures by assessing selection into linkage, agreement between data sources, and the implications of measurement differences for associations with mental health. Using Wave 4 of the UK Household Longitudinal Study linked to Credit Reference Agency (CRA) records, we compared 8 debt types across 3 analytical samples: the full analytical sample, nonconsenters sample, and linked consenters sample. Agreement between self-reported and CRA-recorded debt was assessed using misclassification, sensitivity, specificity, positive predictive value, and negative predictive value. Mental health associations were estimated using Bayesian hierarchical regression models. Respondents who consented to linkage were more likely to be older, partnered, employed, homeowners, and UK-born. Misclassification varied across debt types and was lowest for overdrafts, personal loans, and retail finance. Substantial discrepancies were observed between self-reported and CRA-recorded debt prevalence, particularly for credit cards and other running debts. Differences between data sources also influenced estimated debt-mental health associations. These findings underscore the need to account for selection and measurement error in linked debt data.

Score-based likelihood ratios for authorship verification with authorship embeddings

Rachel Longjohn; Kai Nelson; Padhraic Smyth

卷期
V · I—
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnag115
证据层级
CROSSREF_METADATA

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Score-based likelihood ratios for authorship verification with authorship embeddings

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag115 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

We consider the problem of authorship verification (AV) in forensic science, with the goal of assessing whether two digital documents were written by the same author or two different authors, focusing primarily on short documents, such as social media messages, for which it may be challenging to extract traditional stylometric features. Recent work in deep learning has used data-driven neural network approaches for learning features that distinguish between authors, training a mapping from a document to a continuous-valued vector representation, which we refer to as an authorship embedding. In this paper, we investigate incorporating pre-trained authorship embeddings into a score-based likelihood ratio approach (SLR) for forensic AV for short documents. We investigate the feasibility of calculating SLRs based on these embeddings on several AV datasets, comparing them to a subselection of approaches that use manually-defined numeric features of the text. We find under our experimental settings that relative to the baselines we tested, the embedding-based SLRs are more accurate and have stronger discriminative performance. Based on these preliminary findings and the potential advantages of pre-trained methods in low data regimes, further exploration of the embedding-based approaches to forensic AV for short documents is warranted.

Bias meets Bayes: Bayesian aggregation for peer and self-ranking

Haoyao Ruan; Kaiwen Wang; Johan Lim; Xinlei Wang

卷期
V · I—
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnag122
证据层级
CROSSREF_METADATA

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Bias meets Bayes: Bayesian aggregation for peer and self-ranking

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag122 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Rank aggregation, which aims to combine multiple ordinal rankings into a single, reliable ranking, is widely studied in diverse fields. In academic and professional settings, peer- and self-evaluations play a pivotal role and offer insights into both individual performance and the competence of rankers. However, subjective biases can lead to unreliable or unfair outcomes, especially in a small sample setting. Despite the broad applications of rank aggregation, existing methods often fail to address nuanced biases and the special structure of such data. This paper introduces BayeSPeer, a novel Bayesian hierarchical model designed to jointly estimate latent performance scores and self-assessment biases in peer- and self-ranking data. Through comprehensive simulation studies, BayeSPeer demonstrates superior performance in rank recovery and top-k identification under various levels of sample size, ranking quality, and bias magnitudes. Real-world applications in classroom peer evaluations also highlight the model’s interpretability and practical utility. Our findings showcase the advantages of incorporating structured bias correction rather than omitting self-evaluation, offering a statistically principled and transparent approach to bias-aware ranking in educational and other evaluation contexts.

Measuring frailty in older adults: an indicator based on a super-classifier

Sara Rebottini; Margherita Silan; Pietro Belloni

论文题名

Measuring frailty in older adults: an indicator based on a super-classifier

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag121 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Identifying frail older adults in an ageing population is essential for improving healthcare services. This study proposes a composite indicator to assess individual frailty levels using administrative healthcare data. Given the complex and multidimensional nature of frailty, a multi-outcome approach is adopted. Following an extensive literature review, a set of adverse health events is selected as proxies for frailty. These events were modelled using logistic classifiers, with frailty determinants (associated to adverse health events, selected using a gradient tree boosting) serving as covariates. The sensitivity and specificity of each classifier is used to compose their combined likelihood. From this, we derive an indicator capable of quantifying frailty across the population. The indicator shows robust performance across multiple outcomes and over time. Its primary innovation lies in allowing the use of diverse and outcome-specific sets of frailty determinants without any structural constraint. Overall, we offer an effective tool for quantifying frailty among older adults, potentially supporting health authorities in the prevention of frailty-related adverse events.

Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · ONLINE_FIRST

Bayesian local clustering of age-period mortality surfaces across multiple countries

Giovanni Romanò; Emanuele Aliverti; Daniele Durante

卷期
V · I—
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnag113
证据层级
CROSSREF_METADATA

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Bayesian local clustering of age-period mortality surfaces across multiple countries

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag113 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Although classical mortality modelling literature has focused on single countries in isolation, recent contributions have progressively moved toward joint statistical models for multiple countries. Besides favouring borrowing of information to improve age-period forecasts, this perspective has also potential to infer local similarities among countries’ mortality patterns in specific age classes and periods, which could unveil unexplored demographic trends, while guiding the design of targeted policies. Advancements along this direction are undermined by the lack of multi-country models capable of incorporating the core structures of age-period mortality surfaces together with clustering patterns among countries that are not global, but rather vary locally across the different combinations of ages and periods. We cover this gap by developing a Bayesian model for log-mortality rates that characterizes the age structure of mortality through a B-spline expansion whose country-specific dynamic coefficients encode both changes of this age structure across periods and also local clustering patterns among countries under a time-dependent random partition prior for these country-specific dynamic coefficients. While flexible, this formulation facilitates tractable posterior inference leveraging a suitably designed Gibbs sampler. The application to mortality data from 14 countries unveils local similarities which highlight both previously recognized demographic phenomena and also yet unexplored patterns.

Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · ONLINE_FIRST

Women’s rights and global development policy

Anna Minasyan; Gabriella R Montinola

卷期
V · I—
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnag116
证据层级
CROSSREF_METADATA

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Women’s rights and global development policy

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag116 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

This study examines the effects of gender-marked bilateral development aid on women’s legal rights and economic participation in aid-eligible countries from 1990 to 2021. Using a panel dataset that combines gender-marked aid flows with data from the World Bank’s Women, Business and the Law database, we employ two complementary empirical strategies—a panel fixed effects model with unit-specific trends and a shift-share instrumental variable approach. Our results indicate that gender-marked aid contributes to legal reforms that expand women’s economic rights. We also find evidence of a positive link to women’s employment in the industrial sector, while finding no comparable effects in the services or agricultural sectors. Overall, the findings suggest that targeted development aid can play a significant role in advancing gender-equal legal frameworks and promoting women’s economic participation.

Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · ONLINE_FIRST

Blockchain frontiers in Statistics

Bhojnarine R Rambharat; Yulia R Gel

卷期
V189 · I2
门控
NEW_ARTICLE
DOI
10.1093/jrsssa/qnag035
证据层级
CROSSREF_METADATA

官方论文页面doi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 论文身份与出版状态证据 DOIdoi.org · 2026-11-26 12:19 CST · 规范化论文身份

摘要（可展开，供朗读/NotebookLM）

论文题名

Blockchain frontiers in Statistics

期刊：Journal of the Royal Statistical Society Series A: Statistics in Society · DOI：10.1093/jrsssa/qnag035 · 摘要来源：Crossref 元数据

Opportunities for statisticians in the age of blockchains are now beginning to unfold, with researchers pursuing bold solutions to heretofore unsolved problems with this technology. While the notion of an ‘immutable record’ is not novel, given the advent of modern computing, new methods to transact and maintain robust system of records arise out of blockchains. Analytical challenges from blockchain ecosystems can substantially benefit from statistical methodologies. Inferential methods can be deployed to gain insights about aberrations in blockchains, thus making this technology more resilient to fraud or other criminal interference. This special issue of Statistics in Society underscores the critical need for statistical expertise in blockchains, particularly as it concerns how our global society embraces this new and accessible method of enabling transactions. Many ‘use cases’ for blockchains involve cryptocurrencies, but other applications also employ blockchain technologies. The nature of real estate transactions, the maintenance of health records, or even the manner in which works of art change hands could all leverage efficiencies afforded by blockchains. The hope of this special issue is to encourage more statisticians to get involved in tackling important problems in blockchain data analysis to advance how society can benefit from this technology.
